                tnsCounterBurda_ru = new TNS.TnsCounter({
                'account':'burda_ru',
                'tmsec': 'lisa_total'
                });
            } catch(e){}
        });

        var tnsscript = doc.createElement('script');
        tnsscript.type = 'text/javascript';
        tnsscript.async = true;
        tnsscript.src = ('https:' == doc.location.protocol ? 'https:' : 'http:') +
